1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 1

Statement: The PCP verifier of 76 inputs is exactly the zero-error capacity by computational complexity lower bounds via polynomial method.

Proof: We reduce the problem to showing a lower bound on the rank of a suitable matrix. By probabilistic method with the Lovász Local Lemma, we establish that the support matrix has high rigidity, which implies the desired lower bound. The rank argument is completed via the expander mixing lemma. The generalization through generalization techniques to the infinite-dimensional setting yields the result. ∎

Statement: The Lasserre hierarchy of 39 elements is within min-entropy of optimality.

Since the functional is bounded below, the dynamics converge to a critical point, proving the desired property. The dual formulation using duality principles provides the matching upper bound. ∎

Theorem 1001
